# Decisions

One line per decision. The most recent one is at the bottom.

- 2026-10-24 · Version: Minecraft 1.20.1 + Forge 47.4.x + GregTech Modern 7.5.3 (pinned) + KubeJS 2001.6.5. It is the latest stable GTM, it has the largest addon ecosystem, and it is where Monifactory, Star Technology, TerraFirmaGreg and GTL live. The 1.21.1 branch has had no stable release since July 2025, and 8.0 is in pre-release with a broken API.
- 2026-10-24 · Multiblocks are written in KubeJS through our own pattern helper, so we can migrate to GTM 8.0 (MultiblockPatternBuilder) without rewriting them one by one.
- 2026-10-24 · Mods pinned with packwiz in `pack/`. Modrinth is preferred. From CurseForge, only mods that allow redistribution or have an official URL get in (for example, FTB's maven).
- 2026-10-24 · Normal world (neither skyblock nor void as a start). Mining by hand is only for getting started and is never required to progress.
- 2026-10-24 · Recipe viewer: EMI, with JEI installed as a base for the mods that only ship a JEI plugin.
- 2026-10-24 · Automatic ore in steam: Steam vein drill (ours). The vein is chosen with a sample that is not used up, and the drill bit wears out by chance. The vein table is the same for the electric drills of the later tiers.
- 2026-10-24 · Automatic wood and plants in steam: Steam greenhouse (ours). The sapling or seed acts as a catalyst and is not used up.
- 2026-10-24 · AE2 comes in complete and can be unlocked entirely in LV (the user asked for it).
- 2026-10-24 · "Working station" is read as Crafting Station (J/EMI edition), pending the user's confirmation.
- 2026-10-24 · The Void and Flatland dimensions are built with a vanilla datapack inside KubeJS, with a travel key that is earned through a quest from the start. The dimension mods available are small and poorly maintained.
- 2026-10-24 · ~~Quests in Spanish~~ → **Quests in English** (change requested by the user). Item names carry `en_us` and `es_es`.
- 2026-10-24 · Quest books from other packs are studied and copied or adapted when the license allows it, with attribution in CREDITS.md: Star Technology (MIT), Monifactory and Nomi-CEu (LGPL-3.0). GTNH (CC BY-NC-SA), TerraFirmaGreg (no license) and GTL (unclear license) are used only as references.
- 2026-10-24 · Tests run on the home PC over SSH, only inside `D:\GregtechGrindless`, with one JVM at a time. There are 2-3 tests per tier (startup, multiblocks, integration), plus a static check that the recipes are reachable.
- 2026-10-24 · GTLCore is out: it is tied to GTM 1.4.4 and its license is unclear. GTL, GT Odyssey and GTNH serve only as sources of ideas, with no copying of code, text or textures.
- 2026-10-24 · Gregicality Science and Gregicality Multiblocks (MIT) can be ported with attribution. From Gregicality legacy and Susy-Core (GPL-3.0), only ideas are taken.
- 2026-10-24 · License for the pack itself: pending the user's choice. Proposal: LGPL-3.0 for the code and CC BY-SA 4.0 for the textures.
- 2026-10-24 · EMI is pinned to 1.1.22: 1.1.24 clashes on the client with AllTheLeaks (a mixin of `EmiPlugin` loaded too early). AllTheLeaks stays.
- 2026-10-24 · Client tests use real software OpenGL (Mesa llvmpipe, in a private copy of the JDK on the home PC), not the LWJGL stub. Embeddium, ModernFix and LDLib do not start with the stub.
- 2026-10-24 · The steam vein drill and greenhouse use no water or fertilizer: the ULV fluid hatch is only made in the assembler (LV). The variants with fluid and with fertilizer come with the LV machines.
- 2026-10-24 · Deep veins (diamond, mica, redstone) are only drilled with the steel drill bit.
- 2026-10-24 · AE2 in LV, hybrid: only its inputs are redone with GT LV machines. The presses come from the forming press, not from meteorites, and AE2's silicon comes from the submerged arc furnace. The rest of AE2 stays as it is.
- 2026-10-24 · The submerged arc furnace makes obsidian, not lava, because the lava it made gave more energy in a boiler than it cost.
- 2026-10-24 · Creature drops have their own recipe type (creature simulator, LV–EV). The models are printed without killing anything; a quest gives the first ender pearl. A fermenter feeds a simulator.
- 2026-10-24 · Nether veins are only drilled in LV, with drilling fluid. End veins are left for HV.
- 2026-10-24 · Mods added in batches and tested on server and client: performance, QoL, storage/logistics and decoration (150 in total). Yeetus Experimentus removes the experimental settings warning; Carry On can't pick up GT blocks; Reliable EMI is out (not compatible with EMI 1.1.22).
- 2026-10-24 · Each overlay test uses its own strip of the Overworld (see AGENTS.md), because the tests run at the same time on the same server.
- 2026-10-24 · GTM maintenance is disabled (`machines.enableMaintenance: false`). Fixing multiblocks by hand now and then is exactly the repeated manual chore that the pack removes.
- 2026-10-24 · GTM's oil is infinite (`infiniteBedrockOresFluids`), and there are also deposits in the Flatland and the Void.
- 2026-10-24 · MV: the ore plant runs the full GT chain in one pass, with the same energy and the same byproducts. It gains steps, buses and space, not throughput. The biorefinery reuses GTM's tower only for organic inputs.
- 2026-10-24 · The ore bottleneck is processing, not extraction: the vein drill always gives more than enough.
- 2026-10-24 · Terralith is out (it makes GT veins disappear in its biomes), and Additional Placements is out (it forces a datapack reload of 10–40 s in every new world).
- 2026-10-24 · HV: our refinery (distills, hydrotreats and cracks in one pass, with a cobalt and molybdenum catalyst) and reformer (naphtha → aromatics + H₂ back to the refinery). GTM's tower and cracker are still there.
- 2026-10-24 · HV: the End key leads to a safe platform, with no dragon. The End brings 4 non-radioactive veins plus the zirconium from the addons; naquadah and pitchblende wait for IV.
- 2026-10-24 · Addon veins: germanite in the Nether and zirconium in the End. The space veins (GCYR) and the `*_tf` ones stay in `veins_excluded.json`.
- 2026-10-24 · Addon rule: no mod processes more cheaply than GT in its era, gives out a material before the era that unlocks it, or lowers our multiblocks. Anything that breaks it is removed or moved up a tier with KubeJS.
- 2026-10-24 · GTM Extended Features: Rock Processing Plant, Synthesis Vessel, universal disassembler and Robust Alloy Materializer are out (they pull metal out of stone, skip the platinum plant, or process more cheaply than GT). Large Cracking Machine, Large Pyrolysis Oven and Expanded Assembly Line still have no recipe until IV.
- 2026-10-24 · Tag leaks: if a block from another mod is in a GT material tag and unlocks that material before its era, it is removed from the tag (`integration/tag_leaks.js`). The first one: ExtendedAE's silicon block gave GT silicon in LV.
- 2026-10-24 · AE2 addons: ExtendedAE and Expanded AE with HV circuits (the large coprocessors, EV to LuV); MEGA comes in through a processor made in the IV circuit assembler. Modern AE2 Additions is out (mixin clash).
- 2026-10-24 · EV: the platinum plant reads the recipes loaded in the game and is built from them. GT-- swaps GTM's platline for one in the GTNH style, and the plant uses that one, with the same energy and the same throughput.
- 2026-10-24 · IV: naquadah plant, IV vein drill with the radioactive veins, and a power station with 4 extreme engines (131,072 EU/t). The first fusion is GTM's and stays at LuV.
- 2026-10-24 · Content: from EV on, each era gets 2–3 new multiblocks of ours, and LV and steam gain a few more. The rule "separations are ours, syntheses are GTM's" (translated from Spanish) is relaxed. Each multiblock has to gain something (the user asked for it: lots of content per tier). The "2–3 per era" figure was replaced by the ×1.35 target (2026-10-24, below).
- 2026-10-24 · Change of course (the user asked for it): the pack inherits the `grindless-earlygame` campaign (T0–T15) as its script, but not its machines or systems. It ends at the black hole at the center of the galaxy: you have to get there and make it explode, and the universe with it. The first rocket and the Moon arrive at the end of HV. EV is frozen until the Moon works, and `ev.md` and `iv.md` will be revised along with the journey.
- 2026-10-24 · GT Community Additions: the greenhouse, the recyclers and the scrap box (it gave a random item from any mod), the polymerizer, the industrial coke oven, the chemical generators, the extreme heat exchanger, E85 and dimethyl ether are out: they process more cheaply than GT, or they produce energy or metal out of nothing. The Nether star from UU matter is also out.
- 2026-10-24 · The mega chemical reactor and mega cracker from GTCA, and GT--'s Maceration Stack, move to IV. The IsaMill and the flotation cell move to ZPM.
- 2026-10-24 · GT--'s platinum and naquadah lines stay, because our plants read their recipes. Its 24 `pgs_from_*` are out; they gave platinum from copper and nickel ores in LV.
- 2026-10-24 · GCYR is left alone until space is redesigned.
- 2026-10-24 · Tests: what has already passed its test in-game is not tested again (the user asked for it). Content that only uses an already proven mechanism is validated with the validator and static checks. In-game, only what is new, or what changes the mechanism, is tested (AGENTS.md).
- 2026-10-24 · Approved era table (`docs/journey.md`): the pack is a journey from Earth to the Sagittarius A* black hole. Grindless's eras T0–T14 match 1:1 with Steam Age–MAX, and the ending is the MAX project.
- 2026-10-24 · Moon, option A: titanium enters at EV and comes only from the Moon (lunar ilmenite). It is removed from Earth: the ilmenite from the End vein and the rutile from bauxite.
- 2026-10-24 · HV without titanium: machines, hulls, drill bits (the HV drill bit switches to stainless steel), multiblocks, and the rocket and GCYR suit parts are made with stainless steel.
- 2026-10-24 · The End moves to MV, without ilmenite (it goes to the Moon) or scheelite (it goes to Mars). That way HV only opens the Moon.
- 2026-10-24 · Validator: nothing in an era can require a resource that comes out in a later era.
- 2026-10-24 · Kardashev I in ZPM, II in UHV and III in OpV.
- 2026-10-24 · Travel: the first visit to each planet is made by rocket. After that, people use Waystones or FTB's /home, at no extra cost. Making travel free is intentional.
- 2026-10-24 · AE2 is the backbone of transport between planets: materials, fluids and energy. The quantum bridge between planets has one half built at each end, and the network between planets improves with the eras. Ender chests and ender tanks do not cross to another planet.
- 2026-10-24 · Quests: one tab per era (Steam Age → MAX) that tells the journey, and separate tabs for the quality-of-life mods: storage, AE2 and addons, Productive Bees, Create/Greate if they stay, Waystones/FTB… Quests from packs whose license allows it are reused; otherwise they are written. All in English.
- 2026-10-24 · Post-UHV content: first reuse what exists in external packs, Gregicality, and older packs whose license allows it, including textures and code. Building it from scratch is the last resort.
- 2026-10-24 · Autonomous work (the user asked for it): the orchestrator decides, notes it here and moves on. It only reports if something truly blocks progress.
- 2026-10-24 · HV without titanium, how it is done: in GTM 7.5.3 the HV tier already uses stainless steel (machines, hulls, components and multiblocks), so no GTM recipe is touched. The HV drill bit becomes stainless steel; the titanium one stays registered for old worlds, hidden in EMI, and converts 1:1. The first rocket and the GCYR suit are HV (`integration/gcyr.js`): a scanner with an HV hull, Kapton K instead of polybenzimidazole in the space fabric, fiberglass in the HV chemical bath, and a chest piece with a stainless steel cell. GCYR's oxygen spreader is still in IV: on the first trip, the chest piece's tank is enough.
- 2026-10-24 · v0.4.1, transition until the Moon arrives in v0.5.0: the ilmenite stays in the End's bauxite vein (titanium needs a source until then), but that vein's sample is made only with bauxite and aluminium. The scheelite vein leaves the End with its recipes (`veins_excluded.json`) and returns on Mars; its sample stays registered, hidden and convertible into an empty probe, for the saves in progress; nothing from the Steam Age to HV needs it, and lithium still comes from the salt vein.
- 2026-10-24 · Playable channel (the user asked for it, so they can play while it is being developed): the `jugable` branch only moves forward to tested versions, and a Prism instance imported once (version `canal-jugable`) updates itself on startup. Saves are kept: no registered items are removed without a conversion. Backups are made by FTB Backups 2, which was already in the pack.
- 2026-10-25 · Energy boost (the user asked for it, "a bit permissive like GTL" in `CREDITS.md`, translated from Spanish): in `pack/config/gtceu.yaml`, small boilers ×8 and the cap on large ones ×8 (steel at 12800, like GTL), with heating: bronze 1 / steel 2 / titanium 4 / tungstensteel 8; `steamPerWater` is left at GTM's value. Turbines and fuel: `server_scripts/grindless/power/generators.js` doubles the duration of every generator recipe (steam, combustion, gas, semi-fluid, plasma, GT--'s naquadah reactor and its large version, GT--'s rocket engine, GCYR's Dyson controller) without touching fuel, inputs or EU/t: same fuel, twice the energy, half the consumption for the same power.
- 2026-10-25 · Wireless energy in LV (the user asked for it, "like in Leisure", translated from Spanish): ExtendedAE's wireless connector (`expatternprovider:wireless_connector`) moves from an HV circuit to LV in `integration/expatternprovider.js`; the rest of its recipe (engineering processor, wireless receiver, sky dust) was already reachable in LV. The EU P2P tunnel (`eup2p:eu_p2p_tunnel`) already worked without changes, adapting to a GT cable. GTMUtils' WEB hub/receiver stays in UHV: it crosses dimensions, and it is the part the Moon's AE2 bridge is designed around.
- 2026-10-24 · Create and Greate are out of the pack (the user has doubts and the orchestrator confirms it). They have no role here: they don't process ore, their energy doesn't move GT, AE2 already does the logistics and autocrafting, Greate duplicates GT machines and lets recipes leak, and they cost performance. By the same criterion, the train mod batch, Integrated Dynamics, Botany Pots, Productive Trees, Botania and Occultism are out. A mod only gets in if it makes sense for the journey and is interesting.
- 2026-10-24 · Tools: an early quest gives the full set of GT neutronium tools, unbreakable (the user asked for it), to remove the grind of remaking them. The tools are still crafted with their normal recipe.
- 2026-10-24 · Quest book redone (feedback from the user: texts that sound like AI, a strange order and badly placed dependencies). Books from packs whose license allows it are studied and adapted; there is a style and layout guide (`docs/quests.md`) and a layout checker. The IDs of the quests that are kept do not change, so progress is not lost. The opening explains step by step up to bronze and steam.
- 2026-10-24 · What cannot be obtained is not shown (feedback from the user). The validator classifies each material:
  - A: we give it a source (perlite, from the Nether in LV, whose electrolysis automates obsidian dust).
  - B: its era does not exist yet; it is hidden in EMI until it arrives (table `hidden_until.json` and a constant, "implemented until", translated from Spanish). Most depend on IV's naquadah.
  - C: junk from mods that can never be obtained; it is hidden and its recipes are removed, but its IDs stay registered.
- 2026-10-24 · GCYR stays at 0.2.9 (the development version fixes nothing). The Earth–Moon–Earth flight works with a real player. The scanner bug (#122: a tower that grows after it forms, and an asymmetric platform) and the rocket that burns its pilot are fixed with our own scripts in `space/`. The freeze when teleporting (#91) does not reproduce.
- 2026-10-24 · v0.5.0, the Moon: the lunar drill (`gtceu:lunar_drill`, HV, 8 EU/t per run and a cap of 32, like HV) is a landing module (a stainless steel tripod on regolith, a clean stainless steel body, light-sensor wings and a mast), with its own recipe type (`gtceu:lunar_drilling`) and all its recipes with `.dimension('gcyr:luna')`: it forms anywhere and only drills on the Moon. Table `veins_moon.json`: ilmenite (the only titanium in the pack), dry, with the HV drill bit, and regolith, which gives helium-3 by day (5 mB per run; EV's regolith cell takes over). Samples: two items from each vein, brought by hand on the first trip.
- 2026-10-24 · Energy on the Moon: nothing burns in a vacuum. The first drill runs on batteries brought from Earth; with the bridge, on Earth's energy through EU P2P tunnels (eup2p), or on FE from Applied Flux.
- 2026-10-24 · Earth-Moon bridge: AE2's explosion ritual, with a singularity and a lunar quantum seed (`grindless:lunar_quantum_seed`, HV chemical reactor only on the Moon: ender pearl + 250 mB of helium-3), gives the marked pair `grindless_planet: moon`. A quantum link chamber outside Earth ejects, within one second, any singularity that is not from its planet. The LV ritual pairs still work inside Earth. While the bridge is linked, the Moon's network runs on Earth's AE energy; without it, it needs its own.
- 2026-10-24 · Quality of life is not limited between planets (the user said: "it is a QoL mod, precisely for that reason", translated from Spanish): ender chests, tanks, bags and drawers, GT's and GT--'s ender covers, Modular Routers and Waystones, plates included, work on every planet as they come. This voids the earlier decision that ender chests and ender tanks do not cross to another planet. People travel for free: Waystones with no XP cost and 10 FTB Essentials homes in new worlds.
- 2026-10-24 · Titanium comes only from the Moon: out are the ilmenite in the End's bauxite vein, the rutile as a byproduct of bauxite and aluminium (now bauxite, hematite and gallium), the titanium outputs of the metal mix, the sapphire and ruby sludges, the electrolysis of bauxite, GTCA's bastnasite line and the red mud (`offworld_only.json`). Titanium and ilmenite honeycombs are only processed on the Moon. Helium-3 comes only from regolith (out are the helium separation and the helium-3 from the Nether's liquid air); the tungstate from the End's stone dust waits for Mars.
- 2026-10-24 · GCYR's veins are out (they were Earth materials; the lunar one even had ilmenite): each planet brings its own in its era. On the Moon, only the lunar ilmenite vein generates (ilmenite, olivine, chromite and iron).
- 2026-10-24 · The End in MV (v0.5.0): the End recipes that use the vanadium drill bit move to `vein_drilling/mv/` (the MV vein drill runs them with drilling fluid), and the blaze and the eye-of-ender bath move to MV. The trip is still worth it: aluminium ore, vanadium, chromite and gold, platinum and palladium, and zircon.
- 2026-10-24 · The quest book team writes the Moon and End-in-MV quests from a list with the IDs, the order and what the player does.
- 2026-10-24 · Quality-of-life mods (approved by the user): Polymorph (recipe conflicts), Not Enough Recipe Book, Akashic Tome, Traveler's Titles (the dimensions are named as in the journey: the Overworld is Earth), Building Gadgets 2 and Tool Belt. The last two come from CurseForge, which allows redistributing them.
- 2026-10-24 · HV: syngas plant (`gtceu:syngas_plant`, type `gtceu:syngas`). It gasifies with steam, coal, coke, charcoal or powdered carbon, and the refinery's heavy fuel. It shifts CO with steam over an iron and chromium catalyst, and separates H₂, CO and CO₂, each gas to its own hatch. It also turns the excess CO in HV into H₂ (silicon, wood gas, Nether air). Recipes are based on real chemistry with GTM's units (steam counts as water, one mole per 1,000 mB; each recipe balances C, H and O), and the energy of the stages is summed. It does not reform methane (GTM already does that) and does not touch the reformer, which still runs on naphtha. CO₂ is inert in GTM: it can be discarded or used.
- 2026-10-24 · Quest book: every ID (chapter, quest, task, reward) has 16 hexadecimal digits, and the first one is between 0 and 7. FTB Quests 2001.4 reads IDs with `Long.parseLong(hex, 16)`: one that starts with 8–F gets a random ID on every load (its progress is lost), and every dependency pointing to it disappears. Those were the floating quests the user saw: half of the book's IDs. `tools/quests/fix-ids.mjs` clears the high bit (deterministic, it can be run chapter by chapter); the linter flags it as an error, and `gg_test_quests` counts the loaded dependencies of each chapter.
- 2026-10-24 · Neutronium toolbox (Welcome, asked for by the user): the 17 non-electric GT neutronium tools, the naquadah mortar, and the rubber mallet and rubber plunger (they don't exist in neutronium). Each reward carries the NBT of `IGTTool#get()` and `Unbreakable: 1b`, the root tag that GTM reads in `ToolHelper.damageItem`; neutronium already has it. Tested on a real client: they are not used up, the mining pickaxe and hammer mine (3x3), the magnet collects, and the description shows "Unbreakable" with no durability lines.
- 2026-10-24 · Quest book and v0.5.0: the trip to the End goes in the MV chapter; the rocket and the arrival on the Moon close HV; a new EV chapter (`ev_age`) starts at the lunar base with what exists today (samples, lunar drill, titanium, helium-3, AE2 bridge, energy from Earth, and the EV parts that need titanium). The rocket quests are split by part (engines, tanks, platform, scanner, suit, fuel) so that recipe changes don't break them.
- 2026-10-24 · Quest book: the narrative spine from Steam Age to HV is the chain of observatory charts (Steam Age draws the Moon, LV photographs it and calculates its orbit, MV picks the landing site by radar, HV flies). A new machine's quest only enters the book once its test passes.
- 2026-10-24 · Steam Age: steam blast furnace (`gtceu:steam_blast_furnace`, type `gtceu:steelmaking`). 4 iron ore (raw or crushed, or iron) + 4 coke + 1 calcite → 4 steel + 1 ash, with 4 parallels and about 57 mB/t of steam. It gains steps (the furnace and the primitive blast furnace in one) and speed (like 6 or 7 primitive blast furnaces), not throughput: 1 steel per ore and 1 coke per steel, like GT. GTM's ash acts as slag. The controller needs the primitive blast furnace and steel.
- 2026-10-24 · Steam Age: coking battery with byproducts (`gtceu:coking_battery`, type `gtceu:coking`). 1 coal → 1 coke + 500 mB of creosote + 125 mB of coal gas, with 4 parallels and about 28 mB/t of steam. Coke 1:1, and creosote as in the coke oven; the gas is half of what the MV pyrolysis oven gives (the other half heats the ovens). Per coal, it sits between the coke oven and the pyrolysis oven. The gas is kept for LV (gas turbine, distillery).
- 2026-10-24 · GTM's ULV fluid output hatch can be handcrafted in steam (ULV hull + glass + 2 sticky resin): no steam part takes fluids out of a steam multiblock, and the coking battery needs one. The steam vein drill and greenhouse do not change shape.
- 2026-10-24 · Strengthened LV: sulfide roaster (`gtceu:sulfide_roaster`, type `gtceu:sulfide_roasting`). It does GTM's EBF roasts (sphalerite, pyrite, galena, stibnite and tetrahedrite) with the same solids, the same energy and the same time, but with air (5 × the oxygen) instead of separated oxygen. It converts SO₂ into Sulfuric Acid in the same pass, over a vanadium catalyst that wears out (2%), and returns steam (3,200 mB per 1,000 of SO₂). It also burns loose sulfur, with a negative energy balance.
- 2026-10-24 · Strengthened LV: heliostat tower (`gtceu:heliostat_tower`, type `gtceu:solar_steam`), the first solar power plant. 28 silver mirrors (`grindless:heliostat_mirror`) and a receiver: water → 800 mB/t of steam, only by day, with open sky and no rain (the same check as GTM's solar boiler), 16 EU/t and no overclock. By day it matches a large bronze boiler without fuel; at night it is covered by GTM's multiblock tanks. That sun rule is the one that will govern the bases on the Moon and Mercury.
- 2026-10-24 · Road to the first rocket, MV: engine test stand (`gtceu:engine_test_stand`, type `gtceu:static_fire`). It qualifies GTM's power thrusters four at a time, with RP-1 (diesel → kerosene → RP-1, from GT--) and oxygen, with no chance involved, and the exhaust (CO₂ and water) goes to two hatches of its own. It stays in MV: its MV job is the part the HV engine asks for. In HV, with one HV hatch or two MV hatches, it fires the untested engine with 500 mB of rocket fuel: GCYR's basic engine only comes out of here (its assembler recipe is removed).
- 2026-10-24 · Road to the first rocket, HV: composite autoclave (`gtceu:composite_autoclave`, type `gtceu:composite_curing`). It cures with epoxy and nitrogen (which is vented) the fiberglass ablative tiles, the engine base heat shield (2 per untested engine, instead of 2 of the 6 Kapton K plates), and the para-aramid-wrapped tanks on a stainless steel barrel (2 per basic tank, instead of the 2 barrels). No GCYR rocket block changes; all changes to its parts are in `integration/gcyr_launch_road.js`.
- 2026-10-24 · The radio telescope's lunar landing chart (`grindless:lunar_landing_chart`) goes into GCYR's target chip, which leaves it in the crafting grid (it is not used up), and not into the scanner: the chip holds the target and each launch asks for one, and `integration/gcyr.js` is not touched. Without an observatory in the pack, the chip keeps GCYR's recipe. No other launch multiblock is needed: GCYR's scanner and platform are already HV.
- 2026-10-24 · Observatories, the family that tells the journey (Steam Age to MV): clockwork (`gtceu:steam_observatory`), optical (`gtceu:optical_observatory`) and radio telescope (`gtceu:radio_telescope`), each with its own recipe type so that each chart only comes out of its own machine. Chain: Moon draft (steam: at night, clear sky, and the controller at Y 100 or higher) → photo map + ephemerides (LV: at night and clear sky; the draft is not used up) → landing site chart (MV: at night and no storm, rain is fine; the map and ephemerides are not used up). The conditions are GTM's; if they partly fail, the machine waits without losing what it has done. Each chart is made once and copied by hand (original + backing sheet → 2). No chance involved, and 30 s per observation.
- 2026-10-24 · v0.5.0 ships with what exists today (the user asked for it, so they can play and see the book now): the Moon, the new steam multiblocks up to HV, the quality-of-life mods, the item hiding, and the book with Welcome, Steam Age and EV redone. LV, MV, HV and the quality-of-life tabs, redone by six writers in parallel (child sessions with a cheap model), go in v0.5.1. From now on every version is published on GitHub with notes and a Prism zip pinned to that version (the user asked for it).
- 2026-10-24 · Each tier is exponentially more complex than the one before (the user asked for it): more multiblocks of ours, more process lines and more crossings between lines. What was done from Steam Age to HV is not redone; small changes are added to it (byproducts that feed another line, steps that ask for earlier intermediates). From EV on, each era is designed with that growth as its goal.
- 2026-10-24 · Generous energy, closer to Leisure (the user asked for it): boilers give much more steam, and turbines and generators use less fuel. Complexity does not go down: the same fuels are required. After UHV, generation is also generous, with better fuels or more complex systems, never free energy.
- 2026-10-24 · Leisure (GTL) has no permissive license: its zip says CC BY-NC-SA 4.0, CurseForge says LGPL-3.0, its wireless energy mod (GTMThings) is "all rights reserved", and its textures are borrowed. Only ideas are taken, redone with our own code and textures, and its QoL and multiblocks are brought in by era. What clashes with the rules is adapted (the space elevator, only with a base built on the planet; the parallels, with a cap) or left out: void miners, element copiers, the "creative" ending and the coin market (the user's decision). Inventory by era in `docs/research/leisure.md`.
- 2026-10-24 · Our multiblocks per era from EV on: the number grows by about ×1.35 per era (EV 10 or more, IV 13–14, LuV 18…), on top of the lines and the crossings between lines, which also grow. The source pool is the machine catalog, Leisure's ideas and the Grindless campaign. Audit and proposals in `docs/research/complexity.md`.
- 2026-10-25 · New crossings between lines from LV to HV (complexity audit): six additive recipes, without changing any existing multiblock or recipe (notes in `docs/eras/lv.md`, `mv.md` and `hv.md`).
- 2026-10-25 · Large Steam family (idea from GTL, reimplemented): five new steam multiblocks (`gtceu:large_steam_macerator/centrifuge/thermal_centrifuge/chemical_bath/ore_washer`), a 5×5×5 steel casing and 64 parallels (the cap set by the rule "absurd parallels" in `leisure.md`, translated from Spanish), each using GTM's recipe type unchanged. Checked in GTM 7.5.3's code (not just read): all five have real recipes in the ore byproduct chain at 32 EU/t or less, so none needed the electric LV escape that the order had left in place in case the steam cap blocked the whole type. GTL's assembler (AE2 molecular assembler core) stays out.
- 2026-10-25 · Small steam family (idea from GTL, reimplemented): six new steam multiblocks that GTM did not have at this scale (`gtceu:steam_forge_hammer/compressor/alloy_smelter/mixer/chemical_bath/ore_washer`), a hollow 3×3×3 cube (the same shape as the stock steam macerator/furnace) and 8 parallels (`SteamParallelMultiblockMachine`, the cap set by `leisure.md` §4.2), each using GTM's electric recipe type unchanged; the mixer leaves its fluid hatches optional (powder alloys don't need them), while the chemical bath and ore washer always require them. The ULV fluid input hatch is handcrafted, mirroring the output hatch that the coking battery already used.
- 2026-10-25 · The repository is in English (the user asked for it): docs, code comments, tool messages, data notes and commit messages. The only Spanish kept is the es_es game translation (the user's choice). The docs folders and files got English names. The `jugable` branch and the `canal-jugable` release keep their names: the user's installed Prism instance updates from that URL, so renaming them would force a re-import.
- 2026-10-25 · What counts toward the ×1.35 target: only our progression multiblocks, the ones that add a recipe, route or line that did not exist. Parallel versions of machines that already exist do not count; they are quality of life and are counted apart. That covers the 11 steam family multiblocks and, later, Leisure's universal factories or multi smelter. Today there are 5 progression multiblocks in Steam, 6 in LV, 6 in MV and 7 in HV, plus 11 convenience ones in Steam. Rules 7 and 8 of `AGENTS.md` now hold the growth target and generous power, so every session sees them first.
- 2026-10-25 · EV goes from 7 to 11 multiblocks of ours to meet ×1.35 (details in `docs/eras/ev.md`, "Raising EV to the target"): combined-cycle plant (Earth: a gas turbine whose exhaust boils water, about +40% energy per unit of fuel if a steam turbine takes the steam, stopping if nothing does; generous power from a more complex plant), hot strip mill (Earth: hot ingot + water → plates and rods + steam, the freezer and bender in one step), sealed dome (Moon and Mars: the greenhouse family's EV step, with artificial light, CO₂ in and O₂ out, and biomass for the wither; it reverses "no EV greenhouse"), and rare-earth extraction cascade (Earth: Martian rare earths → samarium and the rest, by solvent extraction with kerosene from MV).
- 2026-10-25 · EV foundation (package A1): the shared EV registrations live in `ev/foundation.js` (startup and server), with no machines or recipe types. Sintered Regolith Bricks (`grindless:sintered_regolith`) are made only on the Moon: 4 `gtceu:lunar_regolith_dust` in GTM's electric furnace with `.dimension('gcyr:luna')`, 120 EU/t, 20 s; the regolith cell will sinter faster under the same condition. Lunar regolith is a GT material of ours (`gtceu:lunar_regolith`, dust only, no decomposition) that any macerator makes from GCYR's moon cobblestone (the Lunar Drill's stone), moon stone, mare regolith and moon sand.
- 2026-10-25 · Liquid nitrogen is `gtceu:liquid_nitrogen`: a liquid at 77 K added to GTM's own nitrogen in `materialModification`, the way GTM gives oxygen and helium theirs; the gas stays `gtceu:nitrogen`. Nothing makes it until the cryogenic plant.
- 2026-10-25 · The rare earths of Mars are merrillite (`gtceu:merrillite`, Ca₈(Mg,Fe)(REE)(PO₄)₇), the phosphate that holds them in Martian meteorites and that dissolves in HCl: a KubeJS GT ore material (`gtceu:mars_merrillite_ore` through GCYR's mars prefix) with apatite and tricalcium phosphate as ore byproducts and no decomposition, so only the extraction cascade splits it.
- 2026-10-25 · Methalox (`gtceu:methalox`) is a gas: EV mixer, 1,000 mB methane + 2,000 mB oxygen → 3,000 mB (CH₄ + 2 O₂), 1,920 EU/t for 1 s. The regolith cell's charge (`grindless:molten_salt_charge`, never used up) is 16 calcium chloride + 4 titanium plates + titanium frame + 2 aluminium cables in the EV assembler, with no tungsten. The atmosphere processor's catalyst (`grindless:ruthenium_catalyst`, used up by chance) is 3 sapphire dust (alumina) + 1 ruthenium dust → 4, ruthenium on alumina as in real Sabatier reactors. The Mars Transfer Chart (`grindless:mars_chart`) is only registered; the deep space antenna writes it.
- 2026-10-25 · EV: the platinum plant is in the pack (`gtceu:platinum_plant`, type `gtceu:pgm_refining`, `grindless:platinum_electrode`) as built on 2026-10-24: the same 5×5×7 hall and the same five recipes, composed at load from GT--'s live line (2 attacks, 3 separations), with the energy of the stages and no parallels (GTM's overclock applies). A plan that cannot be composed is a log warning, not an error. The test pins the recipes to their 2026-10-24 figures.
- 2026-10-25 · Generator multiblocks from KubeJS work on GTM 7.5.3 (spike, package A3). The pattern is `startup_scripts/grindless/lib/generators.js`: `.generator(true)`, `.regressWhenWaiting(false)` (mandatory: with GTM's default a waiting run regresses and the same fuel pays EU twice, GTM issues #1633 and #3685), and a recipe modifier of n parallel copies of the fuel recipe, n = min(cap, the dynamo hatches' V×A / EU per run, fuel): same EU per fuel, no overclock. GTM's dynamo hatches are 2 A. The steam-plant fallback for EV and IV generators is not needed.

At its cap (512 EU/t) it equals one HV single-block generator, so its gain is at LV and MV.
- 2026-10-25 · Universal factories (GTL idea, our code; convenience, counted apart): four MV multiblocks that run GTM's own single-block recipe types in switchable modes: `gtceu:universal_processing_factory` (11 modes), `_assembly_factory` (2), `_separation_factory` (7) and `_mixing_factory` (4). - 2026-10-25 · Multi-mode machines: GTM 7.5.3 does not mark the cached recipe dirty when the mode changes, so a machine keeps running the old mode while its inputs still match. Our multi-mode controllers veto any recipe of an inactive mode in their modifier (`alwaysTryModifyRecipe`); the run in progress finishes and the next one is searched in the new mode. Any future multi-mode controller does the same.
- 2026-10-25 · Leisure utility (GTL ideas, our code; convenience, counted apart): advanced multi smelter (`gtceu:advanced_multi_smelter`, MV), which merges different furnace recipes with the same cooking time into one run, with parallels 2^(coil K / 900) as in GTL (4 cupronickel, 8 kanthal, 16 nichrome, 32 RTM, 64 from HSS-G), capped at 64, each parallel costing one electric furnace; GTM's own multi smelter stays the bulk smelter. Lava furnace (`gtceu:lava_furnace`, MV): GTL's makes lava from cobblestone, but here lava in a large boiler is worth far more than any melting cost, so ours only burns it as heat (10 mB per item, 8 at once, no energy hatch); lava is still never manufactured.
- 2026-10-25 · Weather controller (`gtceu:weather_controller`, MV, type `gtceu:weather_control`, Overworld only): circuit 1 clear for a day (4 calcium chloride), 2 rain for half a day (2 salt), 3 thunderstorm for 5 minutes (2 salt + 4 fine copper wire). The weather changes when a run ends, and it waits while the same weather has more than half its time left. Silver iodide is HV here, so it is not used.

- 2026-10-25 · Rare-earth extraction cascade (package B9): `gtceu:extraction_cascade` (EV, type `gtceu:solvent_extraction`, a 3×3×7 line of mixer-settlers in inert casing). Circuit 1, leach: 4 merrillite dust + 3,000 mB HCl → 1,000 mB rare-earth chloride solution + 1,000 mB phosphoric acid + 3 tricalcium phosphate. Circuit 2, extraction: 1,000 mB solution + 3,000 mB HCl (strip) + 1,000 mB kerosene → 1 samarium chloride + 1 neodymium chloride + 2 mixed rare-earth chloride + 950 mB kerosene + 6,000 mB diluted HCl. Both are EV recipes (76,800 and 384,000 EU), about 1.84 M EU per samarium. GTM's own electrolyzer decomposition turns each chloride (4 dust) into its metal, or GTM's rare earth dust, + 3,000 mB chlorine.
- 2026-10-25 · The rare-earth chlorides are GT materials of ours, because GTM 7.5.3 has none: `samarium_chloride` and `neodymium_chloride` (MCl₃), `mixed_rare_earth_chloride` (GTM rare earth + 3 Cl, which goes on through GTM's rare earth dust and its centrifuge) and `rare_earth_chloride_solution` (acid, no components, no decomposition). Martian merrillite splits 1 Sm : 1 Nd : 2 rest per mole, a game choice so that samarium costs steps, not quantity. The cascade takes an output bus and ranges of hatches (1–3 in, 1–4 out, 1–2 energy).

- 2026-10-25 · The cryogenic plant's night discount is a run-start recipe modifier, not a night recipe: GTM 7.5.3 checks recipe conditions on every tick (a night-only run caught by dawn would wait a whole day) and its recipe lookup keeps only one of two recipes with the same ingredients. A run that starts with the sun down (day time 12,786–23,214) and all 12 radiators seeing the sky costs half (960 EU/t); otherwise the freezer's price. `Level#isNight` is not used, because a thunderstorm trips it.
- 2026-10-25 · Regolith Cell (`gtceu:regolith_cell`, type `gtceu:regolith_processing`, package B2), the chemistry of the Moon base: a 5×5×4 hall of sintered regolith bricks (floor and walls, at least 45) around a stable titanium crucible with a ring of nichrome coils under a tempered glass roof; 1–2 EV energy hatches, 1–2 input buses, 1–2 output buses and 1–5 output hatches (one per gas, or one ME output hatch). Crafted with an EV hull, EV electrolyzer, EV electric furnace, 2 EV pumps and 4 EV circuits. Every recipe carries the Moon's dimension condition and runs at 1,920 EU/t with no parallels; the circuit picks the mode: 1 volatiles (16 lunar regolith dust → 2,000 mB hydrogen, 250 helium, 200 nitrogen, 1 helium-3; the trace of water is vented), 2 electrolysis with the platinum anode (16 dust → 7,000 mB oxygen, 3 silicon, 1 iron; 5 ilmenite in the molten salt charge → 3 titanium ingots, 1 iron, 3,000 mB oxygen), 3 sintering (16 dust → 4 bricks, the furnace's energy per brick).
- 2026-10-25 · Chlorine-free lunar titanium costs exactly the Kroll stages it replaces, read from GTM 7.5.3 (rutile EBF once, then TiCl₄, Kroll EBF and freezer once per ingot: 2,703,360 EU per 3 ingots, 1,408 ticks at EV) with the Kroll's yield (5 ilmenite → 3 titanium + 1 iron); the magnesium and chlorine loop is not counted. The anode (`grindless:platinum_electrode`) wears 0.1% per run in both electrolysis recipes; the salt charge is only needed for titanium and is never used up. - 2026-10-25 · Mars' resources are cut on Earth like the Moon's (`offworld_only.json`, now one entry per world under `worlds`): samarium leaves GTM's `rare_earth_separation` and GTCA's `flsamaric_to_ho` (which keeps the holmium LuV needs, leaving the samarium residue line with no Earth input); deuterium leaves both ender air recipes and `hydrogen_separation` is removed (only tritium separation and fusion use it, so it has no source until IV's heavy water); Productive Bees' tungsten, scheelite and tungstate combs process only on Mars, at 1,920 EU/t.
- 2026-10-25 · Site rules of power (B5b): a fuel only burns where there is air. Every fuel recipe of the burning types (combustion generator, gas turbine, semi-fluid generator, GT--'s rocket engine, GTM's small and large boilers) carries one dimension condition per dimension with air: the Overworld, the Nether, the End, `grindless:void` and `grindless:flat` (`data/grindless/tables/site_fuels.json`, applied by `power/generators.js`). GTM ORs the dimension conditions of a recipe (`DimensionCondition.isOr`), so the allowlist leaves out the Moon, Mars, every orbit and any later planet with no more code; reversed conditions cannot do it (ORed, "not the Moon" or "not Mars" passes everywhere). Fuels that carry their own oxidizer burn anywhere: methalox, GTM's rocket fuel, and GT--'s RP-1 and methylhydrazine nitrate mixtures; so does lava (heat, not fire). GTM 7.5.3's small boilers do use recipes (`gtceu:steam_boiler`), so the rule covers them. Steam turbines, plasma and naquadah generators and solar power burn nothing. Scripts that add burning recipes call `global.grindless.power.burnsInAir(builder)`.
- 2026-10-25 · Methalox burns in the gas turbine anywhere (`grindless:gas_turbine/methalox`): 6 mB per 14 ticks at 32 EU/t, 74.7 EU per mB, exactly the 224 EU per mB of the methane it holds (GTM's value, doubled); the mixer's 12.8 EU per mB is the price of carrying the oxygen. On the Moon and Mars it is the fuel that works.

Energy = GTM's stages with no discount (the ender-air gas collector's 5.12 EU/mB for the thin air, water electrolysis, `carbon_dioxide_from_methane` run backwards): 1,920 EU/t, 134 and 27 ticks. - 2026-10-25 · Validator: the dimension conditions of one recipe are ORed, as in GTM (B5b's patch to `tools/analysis/lib/model.mjs`, test `dimension-or.test.mjs`).
- 2026-10-25 · Earth's weather stays on Earth (`space/airless_weather.js`). In vanilla every dimension with sky light takes the Overworld's rain and thunder, GCYR's planets and orbits included, and full rain alone makes `Level#isNight()` true at noon, which is what GTM's daytime condition reads. So rain at home stopped the lunar solar array, the Lunar Drill's helium-3 and the tripod's wings, and let the antenna run by day. Every tick, the rain and thunder of each dimension of the other planets (`planets.json`) go back to 0, so their sky follows the clock alone; Earth keeps its weather. Bench `airless-weather-1`: Earth at rain 1.00 (the void and flat dimensions too), the Moon and Mars at 0.01.
- 2026-10-25 · Radioactives wait for IV on Earth too (ev.md: no radioactives or quantum eyes in EV; found by the EV era rule of the validator). GTM's thorium ore, in the Nether's beryllium vein, gave uraninite as a byproduct, which opened uranium, radon and the quantum eye from LV on: it now gives lead and rare earth (`offworld_only.json` "byproducts"). Liquid ender air, an IV recipe that two EV energy hatches run, loses its radon with its deuterium. GTM's red granite (an Overworld stone patch) loses its 0.25% uranium dust in the macerator. Productive Bees' IV combs (radioactive, osmium, naquadah) get no recipe until IV: GTM's two-hatch rule makes any 7,680 EU/t recipe reachable with two EV hatches, and the radioactive and osmium bees breed from Earth bees. The other ores with a uraninite byproduct (uraninite, pitchblende, plutonium) are radioactive themselves and held back until IV.
- 2026-10-25 · The EV creature simulator, the wither's, takes an HV emitter and an HV sensor, like the deep space antenna: EV's need GTM's quantum eye (radon), an IV resource. The wither stays in EV.

The EV laser engraver and scanner keep GTM's recipe and open in IV: no EV line needs them.
- 2026-10-25 · GTM's HV fluid drilling rig takes tungsten carbide gears, so it arrives with Mars at the end of EV; the MV rig covers Earth's crude and gas until then. No recipe change.
- 2026-10-25 · Combined-Cycle Plant (B7): `gtceu:combined_cycle_plant`, generator type `gtceu:combined_cycle` (`lib/generators.js`, up to 256 runs, no overclock). Every recipe is generated from a live gas turbine recipe: the same fuel, EU/t and doubled duration (the gas turbine's EU per mB), plus distilled water → steam worth 40% of the run's EU at 1 EU per mB of steam (160 mB of steam per mB of water, rounded up). A fuel burns where it burns in the gas turbine (the source's dimension conditions, else `power.burnsInAir`, else none for fuels that burn anywhere, such as methalox). The steam is the only gain and needs a steam turbine: with no room for it the plant does not run, and fluid voiding is refused. Structure: a 5×8×7 stable titanium turbine hall with a firebrick and titanium-pipe heat-recovery boiler and stack. Controller: EV hull + Large Gas Turbine + 2 EV pumps + 4 EV circuits. `gtceu:combined_cycle` stays out of `power/generators.js`'s types (its recipes copy already doubled durations) and of the burning types (it takes its conditions from its sources).
- 2026-10-25 · Hot Strip Mill (B7): `gtceu:hot_strip_mill`, type `gtceu:hot_rolling`. Recipes generated at load from every vacuum freezer recipe of a hot ingot that needs only EU (helium-cooled ingots, 5,000 K and up, keep the freezer) and the live bender or extruder recipe of the product: circuit 1 plate, 2 rods, 3 foil at their ratios; energy = freezer + bender/extruder, no discount; water in, steam out at 2 mB per kelvin of the blast temperature in whole 160 mB, never more steam than the run's EU. 129 recipes for 58 materials today. Structure: a 3×3×9 line of four rolling stands (titanium gearboxes between steel frames) and two water-spray slices under a hood, stable titanium casing. Controller: EV hull + EV bender + Vacuum Freezer + 2 EV pumps + 4 EV circuits.
- 2026-10-25 · Honest steam (B7): a machine with a steam output refuses the controller's fluid voiding in its recipe modifier (`grindless.steam_outputs.no_voiding`), so a full steam hatch stops it instead of the steam being thrown away. Any later machine whose byproduct must have a destination can use the same refusal.
- 2026-10-25 · Accepted energy loop: Mars' atmosphere processor makes methane at 257 EU per mB, and the combined-cycle plant gets about 314 back from it with its steam (+22%; 224 in a plain gas turbine). The loop needs the Mars base, the Earth bridge and two plants on Earth, and it is far below the EROI of automated wood that the energy audit already accepted (rule 8: generous, never free).
- 2026-10-25 · Validator for EV (C1): `tools/analysis/tiers/ev.json` splits the era into `ev` (the Moon base and Earth's fine chemistry) and `ev-mars` (Mars at the end of the era) in `eraOrder`. Nothing in `ev`, the way to Mars included, may need tungsten, merrillite, samarium, the deuterium-rich ice or deuterium (each only from Mars); uranium, radon and the quantum eye can't be obtained before IV (real FAIL checks).
- 2026-10-25 · The validator models the family tier veto of our drills and greenhouses (`vetoAbove`/`onlyTier`; `onlyTier: ""` = only unfiled recipes, the steam family) and checks site rules statically (`kind: "site"`: every recipe of a type or id pattern runs only in, or never in, the listed dimensions; the fuels' five air dimensions included).
- 2026-10-25 · GTM's marble and red granite patches are a world source of the tiers from the steam age (`steam.json` `stones-gt`), as they already were in the obtain audit: without it the tier model missed red granite's uranium.
- 2026-10-25 · Hidden items at EV: `IMPLEMENTED_ERA = 'ev'`, Mars reachable in the obtain audit. Deuterium is B until IV, helium and nitrogen plasma until LuV, GTCA's helium ion until ZPM; GTCA's samarium-terbium branch (terbium, gadolinium) and the Martian rock dust are C. With the radioactive cuts, whatever hangs off uranium, radon or the quantum eye and star is B until IV, attributed to the End's pitchblende vein (its stage goes before the naquadah one). One command regenerates the table: `Invoke-Reachability.ps1 -Tier obtain -SkipDump -DumpDir <dump> -Table`.
- 2026-10-25 · Rule 7's figures follow what was built: EV ended with 11 multiblocks of ours, so IV needs about 15 and LuV about 20 (the "IV 13–14, LuV 18" above came from EV = 10).
- 2026-10-25 · IV's design, raised to 15 multiblocks of ours (`docs/eras/iv.md`, "Raising IV to the target"). It supersedes the 2026-10-24 line "IV: naquadah plant, IV vein drill with the radioactive veins, and a power station with 4 extreme engines": `iv.md` already replaced those with the fission reactor and no home IV drill. The 7 added: heavy-water plant (Earth, the only deuterium source), Venus acid works, fluorspar kiln (Earth, with a new fluorite vein), uranium in-situ leach field (the End, instead of Tungstensteel-bit drilling), Mercury solar works, tritium removal facility (Moon) and fuel fabrication plant (Earth, LEU in Zircaloy). The reactor's heavy water comes out tritiated, and tritium only comes through the removal facility: IV's build removes liquid ender air's tritium and GTM's `deuterium_separation`. GTM's large miners stay as finite automation.
- 2026-10-25 · The EV chapter (`ev_age`) grows to 66 quests (package C2): the spine follows the journey from the Moon to Mars and ends at the IV Machine Hull; the upper half is off-world (Moon base II, the AE2 network, Mars), the lower half is Earth (power, platinum, nano circuits). Far requirements are named in the text, not drawn as long lines.
- 2026-10-25 · EV energy and dynamo hatches come late in EV (MPIC chips and the Extreme Voltage Coil, after the platinum plant and the phosphorus line), as in GTM. Until then two HV energy hatches run EV recipes, and the lunar solar array and the combined-cycle plant are limited by their HV dynamo hatches (2 A each). Accepted: generating at a tier needs that tier's hatches, and early EV power crosses from Earth over the bridge.
- 2026-10-25 · The building dimensions take air from GTM's own gas collector recipe, which gains their two dimension conditions (ORed), not from copies: copies on the same circuit collide in GTM's one-recipe-per-path lookup, and in v0.6.0 one of them hid the Overworld's air (fixed in v0.6.1). The bench test `gg_test_recipe_lookup` now checks every GT recipe against the real lookup: a collision of ours always gets fixed, and addon-vs-addon collisions are accepted in its baseline only when they lose no route that progression needs.
- 2026-10-25 · Fission reactor (IV-A2): `gtceu:fission_reactor`, generator type `gtceu:fission_core` (`lib/generators.js`, up to 4 channels = parallel copies, no overclock, no regress). Cores from `data/grindless/tables/fission_cores.json`, the programmed circuit picks one: 1 LEU in light water 16,384 EU/t per channel, 2 LEU in heavy water 32,768, 3 MOX in heavy water 32,768, 4 breeder 8,192; 400-tick cycles, 2,000 mB water per channel (vented), the assembly a chanced input and the spent assembly a chanced output (50 of 10,000 each: GTM's OR chance logic makes that one every 200 channel-cycles), heavy water out as tritiated heavy water (decision 3). An assembly gives 1.31 G EU (light), 2.62 G (heavy, MOX) or 0.66 G (breeder). Channels = assemblies, water, room and dynamo V×A: one 16 A IV dynamo hatch carries all four (131,072 EU/t). SCRAM, not explosion: no water or no room for its outputs and it stops at the end of the cycle; item and fluid voiding refused. Its recipes are not doubled by `power/generators.js`.
- 2026-10-25 · Fission reactor structure: 5×5×8, a light concrete basemat, radiation-proof casing (GT--'s) walls and lid, four tungstensteel pipe channels around a tungstensteel frame control rod, a computer heat vent over each channel head, and a robust tungstensteel turbine hall (tungstensteel turbine casings and gearboxes) whose 15-cell floor takes every part. Controller: IV hull + IV field generator + 2 IV electric pumps + 4 IV circuits.

Same-result duplicates stay in the guard's baseline. The validator drops every recipe the guard reports unreachable (`lookup_unreachable.json` in the dump), so it no longer counts on recipes no machine can run.
- 2026-10-25 · IV foundation (package IV-A1): the shared IV registrations live in startup_scripts/grindless/iv/foundation.js and server_scripts/grindless/iv/foundation.js and are published as global.grindless.ivFoundation; no machines or recipe types. Every id of the shared table was registered as written, with no clash.
- 2026-10-25 · Heavy water is `gtceu:heavy_water` (D₂O, a liquid) with `DISABLE_DECOMPOSITION`, like GTM's water; its electrolysis is written by hand at GTM's water numbers (1,000 mB → 2,000 mB deuterium + 1,000 mB oxygen, 30 EU/t, 75 s), because GTM's automatic decomposition of a fluid would cost 25 times less than splitting water. Heavy-water concentrate ((H₂O)₄(D₂O)) and tritiated heavy water (D₂O + DTO, GTM's radioactive hazard ×1, like tritium) never decompose.
- 2026-10-25 · Zircaloy (`gtceu:zircaloy`) is Zircaloy-4 at 63 Zr : 1 Sn (1.5 % tin in atoms): EV mixer, 63 zirconium dust + 1 tin dust → 64, then GTM's EBF at 2,100 K (argon optional, 1,920 EU/t) and the vacuum freezer. Zirconium is dust only in the pack (GTCA's End zircon), so the alloy goes through the EBF, as real zircaloy is vacuum-arc melted.
- 2026-10-25 · Iridosmine (`gtceu:iridosmine`, (Ir,Os)) is the only iridium and osmium carrier: an ore with graphite and sulfur as byproducts, no decomposition and no ore smelting, generated only by Mercury's vein (`grindless:iridosmine_vein_mercury`: iridosmine 3, graphite 2, sulfur 1, the dark carbon-rich and sulfur-rich crust MESSENGER found). Hermean regolith (`gtceu:hermean_regolith_dust`) is any macerator's Mercury rock or cobblestone, like the lunar regolith.

- 2026-10-25 · Hot cell (IV-A3): `gtceu:hot_cell` on its own type `gtceu:hot_cell`; the programmed circuit is the mode: 1 enrichment, 2 fuel, 3 reprocessing, 4 radon. Modes 1 and 4 are composed at every load from GTM's live recipes by `server_scripts/grindless/lib/compose.js` (the platinum plant's composer as a library), with the same yield and the energy of every stage summed and spent at 7,680 EU/t: 30 uraninite + 40,000 mB HF → 1 U-235 + 9 U-238 + 40,000 mB F₂ + 20,000 mB water (1,020,000 EU; the UF₆ and the fluorine the hexafluoride step needs stay inside, so the hot cell turns HF into the same fluorine and creates none), and 1 U-238 + 10,000 mB air → 1,000 mB radon (1,926,400 EU; GTM's 8 plutonium ingots come back as dust and are smelted back inside at the electric furnace's EU, so the first radon needs no plutonium). Modes 2 and 3 are ours, in `data/grindless/tables/hot_cell.json`. Voiding is refused: the waste (`gtnn:radioactive_waste`) is contained. No recipe of the type takes a subset of another's ingredients (checked at load).
- 2026-10-25 · Plutonium balance (IV-A3): a spent LEU or MOX assembly gives back 1 Pu + 8 U-238 (PUREX, 4,000 mB HNO₃ → 4,000 mB NO₂ for GTM's nitric acid recipes, 200 mB Xe, 40 mB Kr, 10 % Cs and Nd, 1 glassed waste); a MOX assembly takes 2 Pu dust + 8 U-238 dust + O₂ (net −1 Pu); a breeder takes a Pu metal core and 9 U-238 ingots (metal fuel, so its ingredients differ from MOX's) and its spent assembly gives back 2 Pu + 7 U-238 (net +1 Pu); an RTG pellet (PuO₂ in Pt-Rh foil) and a Pu-Be source (PuBe₁₃ in stainless steel foil) take 1 Pu each. MOX and the breeder use IV-A1's Zircaloy cladding tubes (decision 7); fresh LEU is the fuel fabrication plant's.

Among GTM's IV machines only the electrolyzer (osmium) and the sensor and emitter (iridium) are Mercury-gated. - 2026-10-25 · Correction to the EU P2P line above: an EUP2P tunnel has no cap of its own (it passes the voltage and splits the amperage over its outputs; read in its code by the IV wave B research). - 2026-10-25 · Heavy-water plant (IV-A5): `gtceu:heavy_water_plant` on its own type `gtceu:isotope_exchange`, the pack's only deuterium source. Circuit 1, exchange: 16 `gtceu:deuterium_rich_ice_dust` + 25,600 mB steam, with a 1,000 mB hydrogen sulfide charge → 3,200 mB heavy-water concentrate + 12,800 mB plain water (depleted, inert) + 160 mB distilled water, 1,907 EU/t for 58 t. Circuit 2, finishing: 1,000 mB concentrate → 200 mB heavy water + 800 mB distilled water, 477 EU/t for 70 t. The energy is GTM's price for each step, summed (the melt at the snow block extraction's 512 EU per 1,000 mB, the exchange at the removed hydrogen_separation's 80 EU per mB of deuterium, the finishing at distill_water_large's 19,200 EU per 576 mB) and spent under 1,920 and 480 EU/t. 1 ice → 40 mB heavy water for 340 EU; a full heavy-water reactor needs 90 ice an hour (9 with the Moon's tritium removal facility). Recipes in `data/grindless/tables/heavy_water_plant.json`.
- 2026-10-25 · The heavy-water plant's H2S charge is GTM's non-consumed fluid input (`notConsumableFluid`, chance 0), proven from KubeJS on the bench (`iv-a5-heavy-water-1`): with less than 1,000 mB of H2S in the input hatches the exchange does not start; it never drains the charge, and an overclocked run takes it once, not scaled. No run-start check is needed. The controller shows the charge.
- 2026-10-25 · Heavy-water plant structure: 9 wide, 14 high, 3 deep: a 3×3×10 cold tower and a 3×3×14 hot tower of inert PTFE casing, hollow, two blocks apart, joined at mid-height by a 2×2 titanium pipe casing bridge, and a 1×1×8 tungstensteel pipe casing vacuum column against the hot tower; parts in the towers' feet (14 cells). Controller (crafting table): IV hull + distillation tower + IV fluid heater + 2 IV electric pumps + 4 IV circuits.
- 2026-10-25 · Fluorspar kiln (IV-A6): `gtceu:fluorspar_kiln` on its own type `gtceu:fluorspar_digestion`, the pack's only route from fluorite to fluorine (decision 10). One recipe, one charge: 3 fluorite dust (`gcyr:fluorite_dust`) + 1,000 mB sulfuric acid + 2,000 mB water → 2,000 mB hydrofluoric acid + 8 gypsum dust, 30 EU/t for 64 ticks (1,920 EU). CaF₂ + H₂SO₄ + 2 H₂O → CaSO₄·2H₂O + 2 HF balances exactly: 8 gypsum dust are one gypsum in GTM's units, and the water is the quench that turns the kiln's anhydrite into gypsum. The figures are GTM's own acid attack on a calcium mineral (`phosphoric_acid_from_apatite`) per 1,000 mB of acid: its EU, its pace, its water and its gypsum. The unreacted fluorite is recycled inside, so a charge gives the full 2,000 mB. No dimension condition.
- 2026-10-25 · The fluorspar kiln runs its charges in parallel, not overclocked (orchestrator's decision): up to 256 copies of the charge at once, as many as the inputs, the room and the energy hatches allow (GTM's ParallelLogic, hatch voltage / 30 EU/t), each at GTM's apatite figures and with no overclock. It is our drills' proven `bores` pattern (the EV tripod's) without the overclock step. A charge always costs 1,920 EU, and one IV energy hatch draws 7,680 EU/t at full feed: iv.md's "1,920 EU per 1,000 mB at IV voltage". The fluorite supply, not the kiln, is the real limit.
- 2026-10-25 · Fluorspar kiln by-products: fluid voiding is refused (the scrubber keeps the HF; the hot cell's mechanism) and item voiding is allowed, because gypsum is inert. The gypsum goes to GTM's concrete (`mixer/concrete_from_calcite` and `concrete_from_marble`, 1 gypsum dust per 1,152 mB) and through it to GT--'s radiation-proof casing; the surplus can be voided. The HF goes to the hot cell's enrichment (20 charges per LEU assembly's uranium), the naquadah plant (about 21 charges per 2 naquadah) and PTFE.
- 2026-10-25 · Fluorspar kiln structure and controller: 4 wide × 6 high × 16 deep, 170 blocks: a 3×3×13 rotary kiln of heatproof machine casing round an air bore on three roller stands of steel frames, turned by a steel gearbox on its own frame, and a 3×3×6 HF scrubber of inert machine casing with a column of PTFE pipe casing packing at the hot end. The controller sits in the middle of the cold end; the feed head round it takes 1-2 energy hatches, 1-2 input buses and 1-4 input hatches (acid and water), and the scrubber's ports (the two lower rows of its back wall and the row under the kiln) 1-2 output buses and 1-2 output hatches. - 2026-10-25 · Refusing to void outputs is a helper, lib/no_voiding.js: the recipe-modifier refusal of grindless.steam_outputs.no_voiding plus the same check in the machine's beforeWorking, because GTM 7.5.3 restarts an idle machine's cached last recipe without its modifier, and with voiding on it skips the room check, so one run would start and void what does not fit. The refused start records its reason (RecipeLogic.putFailureReason). The leach field uses it.

That is within IV-A2's assumption; the reactor returns 361x (light water) or 722x (heavy water).

The hot cell, the fission reactor, the fluorspar kiln, the combined-cycle plant and the hot strip mill get the `beforeWorking` line; bench `release-voiding-fix-1` passes their tests (22, 26, 14 and 23 checks) and the lookup guard.

- 2026-10-25 · The tritium removal facility's night: the cryogenic plant's run-start rule. A run that starts with the sun down (day time 12,786–23,214) and all 12 Night-Sky Radiators seeing the sky costs half (3,440 EU/t, 55,040 EU); otherwise the full price. - 2026-10-25 · Quest links in the Processing Lines only import a quest from an earlier tier's chart, one link per imported quest and chart, with no arrow into a link. A loop or return is its own small circle node at the end of the line ("Electrodes, Back", "Back into the Reactor").

No self-hosted GitHub runner (the repository is public): CI runs T0 and the harness checks on GitHub-hosted runners, with no secrets.

- 2026-10-27 INFRA-ORDER: KubeJS sorts scripts by priority only and ties follow the filesystem walk (NTFS order on Windows, hash order on Linux and macOS), so a Linux server loaded zpm/fusion.js before luv/fusion.js. Every script now carries one stamped `// priority:` line 1, derived from the bands in `tools/analysis/script_order.json` and then the Windows NTFS pre-order walk that every test proved; never hand-edited (`tools/analysis/script-order.mjs --write`, run by `Update-PackIndex.ps1`; `--check` in T0). The KubeJS parse hazard check flags only the keys KubeJS acts on (priority, ignored, packmode, requires). Proof: Windows enumeration equal on all three sides (138/286/129); VM run 20261027-230939-server-919 logged exactly the expected order.

- 2026-10-28 · Quality review (owner directive): UIV is parked after B3 (B4, M2, B5, C1-C3 remain) and the whole mod gets a global quality audit before any new tier: EMI visibility (QR-A0), structure design and formed look (QR-B0), controller and part recipes (QR-C0), uncraftable, unused or undocumented items and processes (QR-D0), multiblock loop sounds generated with ElevenLabs (QR-E0; the key is read only at runtime from outside the repo) and a large horizon collector buff (QR-F0). The recipe ladder applies to our content only; GTM's own recipes stay as shipped. The owner lifted the 2-worker cap for this program; the bench stays at the VM's 3 slots. Standards from Phase 0 go into DECISIONS and the scoped instructions, then per-era fix packages apply them, and the next tier is built to them.
- 2026-10-29 · Recipe cost (owner directive): from IV up, none of our items or blocks, nor those of the addon mods (gtca, gtnn, extendedfeatures and similar), may have a crafting-table recipe. This covers controllers, casings and other structure parts, hatches, components and intermediates. The exemptions are basic single-block machines in GTM's hull convention, save-compat 1:1 conversions, unpacking, and cases QR-C0 justifies in docs/quality/recipes.md. IV is machine-made with a fluid; from LuV, controllers, hatches and complex parts use the assembly line. Every era gets an item-complexity pass (chain depth, distinct inputs, a machine of its era, fluid), raised by complexity, never by raw quantity or longer times. GTM's own recipes are only counted; changing them is a separate decision.
- 2026-10-29 · Quality gates (QR Phase 0): each audit leaves a static gate in tools/analysis with a baseline ratchet: `structure-quality.mjs` (QR-B0), `recipe-quality.mjs` (QR-C0) and `process-audit.mjs` (QR-D0). A finding absent from its baseline fails T0, and a fix deletes its baseline entry. New content must pass without baseline entries.
- 2026-10-29 · Structure rubric (QR-B0, docs/quality/structures.md): a multiblock shows its function with frames, glass, pipes, coils or gearboxes as fits; no hollow box, single-block shell or solid cuboid, and no block dominating the volume. Pilots: the five Large Steam machines (a boiler house) and the methane pyrolyser (a tower). Rhino keeps the first iteration's `const` inside loops, which made 13 ZPM and UV structures solid cuboids; pattern builders use `var` in loops and a test enforces it. Those 15 shapes changed (release note).
- 2026-10-29 · Recipe ladder (QR-C0, docs/quality/recipes.md): the owner's no-crafting-table rule from IV (C-MANUAL) plus a form per tier: Steam to HV crafting with domain inputs, IV a machine with a fluid, LuV the assembly line, ZPM the assembly line with scanner research, UV and above the assembly line with station research and an era fluid. Each tier also has a complexity floor (chain depth, distinct inputs, fluids, form, research). `recipe-quality` owns recipe complexity; `process-audit`'s trivial_step covers obtain chains only. Pilots: cryogenic plant, waste retrieval and actinide partitioning plants, electron beam furnace, single crystal foundry and UEV parallel hatch (recipe ids moved from `crafting/` to `assembler/` or `assembly_line/`). GTM's own IV+ crafting recipes are not counted yet (needs a native dump).
- 2026-10-29 · M-INFO (owner directive, QR-D0): every multiblock of ours ships with an EMI info page (en_us and es_es) saying what it does and what it is for; special-purpose machines such as chart or probe stations get their own page. No recipe type is registered before its first recipe. Phase 2 writes the 216 missing pages (docs/quality/items.md).
- 2026-10-29 · Machine sounds (QR-E0, docs/quality/sounds.md): working loops are set per GTM recipe type from `startup_scripts/grindless/sounds.js` (`GTRecipeType.setSound`), so all machines of one type share a loop. They are generated with ElevenLabs on a paid plan through tools/assets/New-MachineSound.ps1, and the key is read from outside the repo. Pilots: observatory, mega chemical complex, plasma quench caster.
- 2026-10-29 · Horizon Collector (QR-F0): one run every 8 s instead of 60 s, up to 4 output stacks per run, flint on circuit 10, 16 EU/t unchanged. A collector placed earlier may keep one output slot: break and re-place it.
- 2026-10-29 · Expanded Assembly Line (owner request, QR-G0): the order-free assembly line from GTM Extended Features is made at LuV in the normal assembly line from a normal assembly line controller. Research reaches it through the LuV wireless data receptor and a transmissor in a Matrix Data Relay holding a data access hatch; all four parts are made in the assembly line. Formation and research are not proven in game: KubeJS can't reach the addon's classes on a dedicated server.

Every client suite now holds until EMI finishes reloading and runs the native coverage audit with no content baseline (missing GT displays, workstations, previews, routes or required info pages fail it); the MEGA preview counts as proven only with its rendered screenshot. `-EmiAuditTimeoutSec` (600-1800) and `-EmiAuditClientXmx` (4G-8G) are opt-in, Linux client-only, and a heap above 4G needs `-Slots 1`; all other caps stay. Pat.frame materials are GTM ids with underscores: B0's `'tungstensteel'` aborted startup (run 20261029-021359-client-a24) and T0 now allows only frame materials seen natively.

- 2026-10-31 · Project Reforge (owner brief): the pause is lifted. v0.17.0 is reworked into v1.0.0 in ten phases (0 rules and diagnosis, 1 cost tooling, 2 cost rebalance, 3 era and line audit, 4 research, 5 quest rebuild, 6 assets and sounds, 7 MAX+ and the Hyperstargate, 8 cleanup, 9 release) on the integration branch ezequiel24123z-project-reforge. Baseline: MAX reachability PASS on run 20261031-224215-server-5aa. Up to 6 nested sessions run at once, never two on the same files (replaces the 3-worker limit).
- 2026-10-31 · Reforge rule 1 (owner): every basic resource also has a renewable, automatable route; hand mining only starts the game.
- 2026-10-31 · Reforge rule 2 (owner): complexity comes first, and quantity is also high and expensive, for items and fluids, above all in key recipes and final items. Up to LV, costs are only reviewed; MV to MAX scale steeply per era; MAX+ goes far beyond MAX. Every input stays automatable, and recipe times are never inflated in place of cost. Supersedes "never raw quantity", "quantity walls are bad" and the docs/eras/mega.md section 5 override. Why: owner, and RF-0D measured flat costs (median 2 to 6 items per recipe from MV to UIV; mean fluid 1810 mB per recipe at IV and 733 mB at MAX).
- 2026-10-31 · Reforge rule 5 (owner): advanced eras get longer, more numerous chapters (Star Technology and GTNH style) that teach every new wafer, circuit generation, process and multiblock; rewards are proportionate and never skip a production line; quests never block recipes. Supersedes "quests are short and give a small reward". Why: owner, and RF-0D found 33 to 58 quests per era with no growth.
- 2026-10-31 · Reforge rule 7 (owner): any era, Steam included, may be reworked when that makes the pack better; the growth figures stay guidance, never quotas. Supersedes "Steam to HV only gets small additions" and docs/research/complexity.md section 4. Why: Phase 3 audits every era; RF-0D found MAX at 10 own multiblocks against a guide of 24, and 46 lines against 68.
- 2026-10-31 · Reforge rule 9 (owner): the pack stays huge and deeply industrial; Reforge removes pointless complexity, never meaningful complexity.
- 2026-10-31 · No save compatibility (owner: nobody has played the pack yet): ids, dimensions, multiblock shapes and quests may change, with no conversion recipes or notices; a removed id takes its references with it; README says to start a new world. Phase 8 candidates: the OpV HT retrofit conversions, the 25 retired save-compat items, and the opv_c3/max_c3 save fixtures with their zones.json notes. Phase 5: the quest lint's "existing saves" exception. Open: whether save compatibility returns after v1.0.0.

- 2026-10-31 · Reforge diagnosis (RF-0D, docs/reforge/00_diagnosis.md) accepted; census via tools/analysis/census.mjs; its top-20 fixes feed Phases 2, 3, 5, 6 and 8. Integrator correction: GTM 7.5.3 keeps every ingredient of a recipe over its type's limits (it only warns). Of the 67 kubejs slot warnings (66 recipes), 50 are capacity-lint errors and 16 exceed the limit only by the programmed circuit, which machines keep in a separate slot (whether EMI shows it is unverified). Limits: 976 table-generated recipes are not attributed to an era; textures are checked by file name; the quality scan covers UEV to UXV only; automatability is not measured. The obtain pass FAILS on 56 unclassified OpV/MAX groups because IMPLEMENTED_ERA in integration/hidden.js is still uxv and tiers/obtain.json has no OpV/MAX places (Phase 3); the staged-release machinery is a Phase 8 candidate.
- 2026-10-31 · Capacity lint (RF-1A, tools/analysis/capacity-lint.mjs, part of T0). A recipe's tier is the lowest voltage that covers its EU/t. The recipe must fit a singleblock of that tier (or the next existing one), or, as a warning only, a multiblock's hatches one tier up. Multiblock item slots are unbounded, each fluid must fit one tank, and the type's max IO is always enforced (GTM only warns). Ratchet: tools/analysis/capacity_known.json lists the 87 current errors (mostly assembler recipes with 2 fluids; also spark plasma sintering, lathe and distillery recipes, and venus_heat's 32 B of steam over a 16 B hatch) and may only shrink. Limits: hatches one tier up is an assumption; one fluid per tank is stricter than GTM's spreading over hatches; runtime-made and GTM-generated recipes are not seen; 37 errors produced no kubejs warning (open). GTM sources were read for semantics only.
- 2026-10-31 · Cost curve (RF-1B with repair 1, docs/reforge/01_cost_matrix.md; tools/analysis/cost-matrix.mjs prices the cheapest acyclic chain to base resources). Measured core index on v0.17.0 (nine core components, base items plus litres): LV 0.19, MV 0.51, HV 0.56, EV 0.82, IV 0.91, LuV 7.9, ZPM 36, UV 168, UHV 519, UEV 1,419, UIV 2,594, UXV 7,937, OPV 242; MAX is unpriceable (tungsten and field_can loop, gtnn circuits without a recipe). Accepted curve: target index 3 x 2.5^n from MV (MV 3, HV 7.5, EV 19, IV 47, LuV 117, ZPM 293, UV 732, UHV 1,831, UEV 4,578, UIV 11,444, UXV 28,610, OPV 71,526, MAX 178,814); MAX+ uses x5 per era in its own row; LV is reviewed, not scaled. Phase 2 applies it from a table as direct per-era factors solved from MV upward and checked with `--compare` until each era is within about 10% of its target; it moves quantities and ingredient counts only, never duration or EU/t; capacity overflows are solved by more steps or ingredients, then bigger machines, only then counts. Matter-multiplying chains (ash separation, tiny-dust splitting, the netherrack_blue_topaz_ore route) are reviewed through excludeRecipes before calibrating. Why: owner rule 2 (MV to MAX scale steeply per era); one rule removes the LuV, ZPM, UV and UEV cliffs and the HV, IV and OPV regressions and never asks an era to get cheaper (smallest multiplier x3.2, UEV). The anchor 3 is a choice: if the HV to IV factors (x13 to x51) prove unworkable, lower the anchor to 2, not the rate.
- 2026-11-01 · Line audit (RF-3C, docs/reforge/03_line_audit.md; tools/analysis/line-audit.mjs over the Processing Lines charts): lines are judged by step count, reach back, destinations, skip route and duplicates; verdicts are leads that each fix package confirms against the era docs. Chart lines sit far below the AGENTS.md:18 guide from ZPM (ZPM 6, UV 8) and UHV to OpV lines have no crossings in the charts: RF-3F to RF-3H first settle whether the chart or the content is short, never by padding.
- 2026-11-01 · Assets audit (RF-6A, docs/reforge/06_assets_audit.md; tools/analysis/assets-audit.mjs): 50 textures and 36 sound files are missing, 54 of 62 sounds are 15-25 dB louder than GTM, and Steam to ZPM have a real visual identity while UEV, UIV, OpV and MAX are tint swaps of shared bodies. Phase 6 works through the generators (RF-6B missing textures, RF-6C controller faces, RF-6D era-style library, RF-6E sound pipeline, RF-6F sound gates; 6E and 6F serial). - 2026-11-01 · MAX+ creative items, Hyperstargate and ending (RF-7A): six creative item kinds in 48 crafts, ingredients of the Hyperstargate only and never placeable outside creative mode (supersedes DECISIONS.md:102's "creative ending left out" for these items only; no game-mode change anywhere). The Hyperstargate is GTNH's Stargate shape at about 10x (1,212 grid crafts, 13,440 frame parts, 15,744 compressed plates, about 155,000 processors, 64 core crystals) with no inflated times; its dial consumes the genome archive and keeps a horizon lock. Crossing it moves the player to `grindless:horizon_city` (ruined futuristic cities, no mechanics, research or blueprints); the first arrival sends WIN_GAME, a title and the `epilogue` book, and the world continues. MAX open questions 1, 12 and 13 and MAX+ open questions 1 to 9 take their defaults until the owner answers; spikes S0-1, S0-4 and S0-7 gate the design.
- 2026-11-01 · Cost-curve mechanism (RF-2A, docs/reforge/02_cost_curve.md): `integration/cost_curve.js` (band -300, after every recipe script) scales the consumed item and fluid inputs of key-item recipes, GTM originals in place and ours through serialize()/set('inputs'), by a factor per era from `tables/cost_curve/<era>.json`; outputs, duration and EU/t never change. Tables landed disabled; each Phase 2 band package switches its eras on. One rule shared by the pack and the Node tools (lib/cost_curve.mjs); T1 20261101-012051-server-42f matched the prediction exactly. The matrix now counts shaped cells (corrected baseline in 02). Default (owner question): the 50 crafting-table recipes of MV to IV core components are removed in the band tables, because a 3x3 grid cannot scale and they bypass the curve.

- 2026-11-01 · Cost re-solve (P2-Z, #116): a scaled count is floor(n x f), plus one when the fraction reaches a fixed per-(id, count) threshold (a dither), instead of ceil(n x f), which took single items from 1 to 2 and left a staircase no factor could fit. Every era from MV to MAX lands within 10% of 3 x 2.5^n (LuV 0.912 and UIV 0.907 near the edge; changing a factor means re-solving every era above it). Factors: MV 1.677, HV 2.285, EV 3.266, IV 3.322, LuV 1.998, ZPM 1.77, UV 2.742, UHV 1.492, UEV 1.443, UIV 1.445, UXV 1.029, OpV 1.427, MAX 1.571.
