- 2026-11-03 · Owner delegation (owner, 2026-11-03: "make the decisions that best fit the pack", publishing on CurseForge excepted): the integrator decides every open item, and these decisions stand:
  - STATUS's open owner questions take their written defaults.
  - M2-15's trivial-step threshold stays.
  - Every wafer is taught in its era chapter (M5b-25).
  - The steel boiler's x7.111 stays, stated at principle 8 (M6-07).
  - Art stays generated: the four MAX controllers and the interferometer get faces of their own, and the 58 loop seams are fixed in the sound generator.
  - The Mirror Shell gets a dedicated, non-active shell coil casing (band D5).
  - The eight upstream structures EMI cannot preview get EMI build pages (A6-03; no Java).
  - Recipe ids lose the doubled type (A2-02).
- 2026-11-03 · Multiblock scale (owner, 2026-11-03): AGENTS.md principle 4 now says multiblocks grow with their tier and their use. From UEV on, their body is the era's own family of casings: expensive, and each needing a product only that era makes. The guide and the casing rules are in docs/quality/structures.md, *Scale by tier and use*.
  - The long-lines bands become D2 (EV to ZPM), D3 (UV and UHV), D4 (UEV to UXV) and D5 (OpV to MAX+).
  - D4 and D5 apply the scale and the casing families; D2 and D3 resize only what contradicts its use.
- 2026-11-03 · Batch 16, Project Reforge's long lines from EV to MAX+ (released as v1.5.0): the decisions of the D2 to D5 packages, the audits and the integrator, one line each.
  - D2-NIOBIUM (#330): IV I4: pyrochlore HF digestion in the fluorspar kiln (CaF2 back as fluorite, kiln itemsOut 2), aluminothermic Nb and Na-reduced Ta in the EBF; GTM pyrochlore electrolysis removed.
  - D2-GERMANIUM (#329): LuV L4: Io c2 chlorination gives GeCl4 and InCl3 only (atom-balanced, 2 Ge + 1 In per run); chlorine and hydrogen loop through HCl electrolysis.
  - D2-FUEL (#318): D2-3 taken (A): IV's uranium front end is loose (liquor, IX, yellowcake, U3O8, UF4, deconversion, Hf-free zircaloy); hot cell c1 re-pinned; UF6 is never electrolysed; fluorine returns as HF.
  - D4-UXV-SPIN (#331): X1 replaces meso_logic_die with the spintronic wafer flow; the DyScO3 substrate is consumed.
  - D4-LINKS (#334): census II/III keep the previous census as non-consumed inputs; the SX master alloy takes 6N Hf; the electroded KTP wafer is the poled crystal (PPKTP dust and its poling row cut).
  - D4-SMES (#333): D4-9: the OpV SMES cell is rewound from four UXV cells.
  - D5-ALA (#324): the array's and Hyperfactory's data hatch sits in an optional cell D (max 1) for the EMI preview; GTM's ResearchCondition always passes, so research is enforced by a beforeWorking veto.
  - D5-CAS-MAXP (#332): the gate, Fe-56, cryostat and window casings never take horizon plates or condensate; iron-56 is a gtceu material from the Fe-56 cask.
  - D5-SHAPE-OPV-A/B (#327, #335): the spallation station merges into the Antiproton Complex; OpV SGR/Terzan 5 machines 540-2,549 cells on lamina; the field-frozen press is the field-frozen casing's bootstrap body.
  - D5-SHAPE-MAX-A (#337): H-2 built: the Mirror Shell is a 35-box two-layer sphere of 7,060 cells with 26 glass ports.
  - D5-SHAPE-MAXP-A (#338): H-1 realised: the Hyperstargate is a 59 x 60 x 23 circle of 9,385 cells; the open plane is the disc inside the ring.
  - D5-QPU-OPV (#336): the Si-28 line gains the SOI anneal, the tetrachloride loop and the purge with honest destinations.
  - D4-SHAPE-UXV-A (#328): gum, mu-metal and pasta casings are barred from machines on the UXV hull chain.
  - AUD3-ENDING (#326): the Hyperstargate arrival releases only the chunk tickets it added.
  - Casing families are their era's bootstrap material: held to VA[N-1] and tier N-1 components (recipe_quality familyCasings); machines reach VA[N] on two N-1 hatches (tier variants @<tier>2).
  - Bootstrap bodies: a machine whose product a family casing needs never contains it anywhere (body, booth, accents); it takes the previous era's casing of the same job. UEV: CVI furnace, SiC fibre plant (R, H, M), EB furnace (R, M), megabar (M to H). UIV: levitation melter + uiv/shapes.js T.BOOT (6 machines). OpV: field-frozen press.
  - The superhydride magnet casing takes no bonded Fe16N2 magnets (Barnard-only), so Earth's UEV machines can be bodied in it (era rule).
  - A window counts as a bulk structural part in recipe quality, like glass.
  - OpV palettes: a bare material name is a GT frame.
  - The static KubeJS stand-in records builder durations (composed recipes).
  - The observatory sky wake reads String(level.getDimension()) (KubeJS 6); it never ran before.
  - Process audit (dump 02f8c745): thoria pellets take a small Nb2O5 sintering aid (IV niobium crossing); ThN pellets sinter under a lost 15N flush; the CMOS flash anneal wears the LaB6 cathode at 1 %; the QPC's kept helium bath is a reasoned key, as laser_cooled_ps.
  - IV fixtures read GT assembler recipes through GT contents; IV's tritium rule forbids GTM's and GT--'s routes, not later eras' own rows.
  - D3-SMALL (#339): the Th:CaF2 crystal takes laser-grade ThF4 (D3-9); the hot cell and fluorspar kiln make it at IV.
  - D2-SIZE-LUV (#340): the microgravity smelter is a 177-cell truss hall with coil bands and a drawing tower.
  - D3-SIZE-UV2 (#341): five UV structures reshaped into halls; the zone tower's RF rings are cupronickel (cosmetic; its rows have no coil temperature).
  - D5-SHAPE-MAXP-B1 (#344): MAX+ stage I at 2.4k-4.9k cells on horizon bodies; the condensate refinery keeps MAX casings (it makes the family's billet).
  - AUD3-RECIPES (#353): the liquid-water shift runs anywhere with a vaporisation stage of 4,800 EU/mol (80 -> 100 t at 480 EU/t, energy-derived); the hydrogen convention is 1,000 mB = one H, so the C-12 methanations take 4,000 and 6,000 mB.
  - D2-CHARTS-IV (#350): the IV chart unfolds the fuel cycle (12 real steps; no padding to the 14 of the design), adds the PBI cluster, the staged platline and niobium/tantalum; the reprocessing Hot Cell keeps key hotcell for the OpV link.
  - D5-CHARTS-OPV (#351): the OpV chart is O1-O9, laid out by lanes; fan-outs became hand-off raws; O6 has 11 steps (the sealed MMIC tile is a real EB-to-assembler step).
  - D3-UPW (#369): the Ultrapure Water Plant climbs GTCA purified water grades 1-5 in six 200 t rows (carbon, RO, EDI, UV oxidation, degassing, mixed-bed polish); 8,000 mB water still gives 6,000 mB; the polish brine is 1,000 mB salt water; grades 6-8 stay hidden (obtain.json).
  - D2-PEDOT (#352): LuV PEDOT:PSS by the industrial EDOT route (14 rows); the bromine loop closes through HBr, ZnBr2 and NaBr electrolysis.
  - D2-MEA (#371): the PEDOT:PSS microelectrode array is made in a cleanroom assembler (the laser engraver has no fluid slot), on a sapphire plate; the LuV NPU takes one array instead of 8 electrum plates.
  - D5-QPU-MAX (#354): the MAX qubit stage is four steps (circuits 14/17/12/4: transducer-bonded stack, photonic tetron module, calibration by nuclear demagnetisation, shielded quantum module); the supracausal mainframe takes only the module route; a failed bond loses one die at 10 %.
  - The antiproton_production loop sound is the Antiproton Complex target hall (loop_antiproton_target_hall); the spallation station is gone.
  - D5-SHAPE-MAX-B (#343): the other 13 MAX structures are 1.9k-3.4k-cell halls on MAX family bodies (max/shapes.js); the membrane line stays free of the membrane vacuum casing (C-4); the winch and interferometer are 45 high.
  - D3-GATERING (#358): the warp gate casing is 1 qualified MgB2 coil + 4 neutronium plates + frame -> 2 (cable, winding, reaction, impregnation, qualification); the magnet works keeps its liquid helium as a charge.
  - D2-APT (#355): EV tungsten runs scheelite -> ammonium tungstate -> APT -> WO3 -> hydrogen reduction (two chemical reactor steps, a distillery and two EBF steps); GTM's tungstic acid electrolysis is retired; the steam of the calcination and the reduction is vented.
  - MAX bootstrap (integrator, dump f88066fda790): the membrane line, which rolls the first MAX circuit's shield, is built with OpV circuits and OpV robot arms and its roller casing with OpV circuits; the qubit stage's TFLN fab, photonic foundry and millikelvin works reach VH[MAX] on two OpV hatches (tiers B16-MAX).
  - D2-MATTER (#363): GTM's diamond electrolysis is retired; the nanodiamond cleaning row conserves carbon and hydrogen (4 nanodiamond + 4,000 mB H -> 3 diamond dust + 1,000 mB methane).
  - D2-NAQPOWER (#362) and D2-4: the naquadah ladder (II 54,932 t, IV 1,431, V 1,669, large reactor 690, final values); GT--'s uranium and thorium liquid fuels are retired (obtain class C) with the thorium implosion chain; GT--'s enriched uranium stays as a route to its neutron source.
  - D3-SRF (#357): the SRF chain gains niobium half-cells (c14) and dressed cavities (c15); the niobium per cavity is unchanged; the cryomodule takes 8 dressed cavities.
  - D2-TAPE (#356): LuV L1 is Mond carbonyl nickel (LCR rows, CO recycled) -> Ni-W alloy -> an eight-step coated conductor table in batches of 20; CeO2 is cerium dust.

  - D3-XENO (#368): the scaffold chain splits into chitin (c6), chitosan (c7, its acetyl back as acetic acid for the scaffold row) and scaffold (c3); like the plant's other rows they are portable processing (only gathering and growing stay on Proxima b).

  - Integrator (UXV): the pasta extractor and the Pasta Mill are walled with the pasta containment casing, as lines-uev-uxv.md section 9 intends (it was in no structure); its FRC cartridges (Cygnus X-1) and cage parts (UIV) are made before Vela.
  - MAX+ crystals (integrator, after AUD3-RATES): no rate change. The horizon crystals are few (four core crystals per creative core, one controller crystal per Hyperstargate) and all three furnace rows run in one machine, so it alternates; about 28 minutes of slurry per core boule; the cost is the 32,000,000 mB of plasma per core boule (principle 2).
  - AUD3-RATES (#305): rate cards for HV, LuV and MAX+ are built from checked claims and verified by pl-check. Balance noted, not changed: HV full-conversion hydrogen beats gasified coal and methanol syngas on every cost (12,000 EU vs 14,400 and 13,200 per bucket), those two exist for their CO; LuV carbonyl iron powder is dominated by decomposition.
  - AUD3-PERF (#306): factory_load baselines (docs/testing/usage.md): 50 ms a tick is the 20 TPS budget; no regression threshold until repeated runs show the noise; heap is not measurable (KubeJS class filter).
  - Quests (#396, #400): a chapter teaches each line once and each new thing once (the MAX chapter: 21 line quests and 38 step quests, not one templated quest per chart step); quest needs come from the dump's recipes, never from the design documents.
  - GTM's highTierContent stays on (TP-S0F, 2026-10-30): GTM registers the UEV to UXV hulls and hatches; fixtures that pinned it off were stale.
  - Integrator (assembly lines, after T2-FIX-LOW #403): the cost curve splits an assembly-line item input scaled past 64 into entries of at most 64, in order, as GTCEu writes its own recipes (the line matches one input bus per entry and an ME input bus slot holds one stack, so the LuV motor's 128 fine ruridit wire never started). 32 inputs in 20 recipes; totals and cost unchanged; the widest recipe stays under the line's 16 buses. Fixtures count a recipe type per era directory, as later eras add rows to shared types.
  - UV: the Starchip Fab works in any dimension (no dimension condition; its clean room is part of its hall); its EMI note said Earth orbit.
  - Fixtures after the reshape (#399, #401 to #404, #406):
    - A fixture on a merged controller selects its recipe type explicitly.
    - Fixtures place shapes from the structure's minimum corner, never a fixed controller cell.
    - Wireless fixtures assert the strongest-cell tier (WL-1); fixtures that share the wireless network run alone.
    - A fixture whose run cannot fit the 600 s native budget skips only the waiting with setProgress (opv_bulge_relay: two boots).
    - The answered S0 spikes on removed test-only machines are retired (UXV machines; OpV machines, pasta and relay 1 and 2).
  - Bench: a run holds its checkout's in-use lock, so another run's cleanup never deletes a checkout in use (more distinct SHAs at once than GG_WORKSPACE_KEEP had cost runs their summary).
- 2026-11-04 · EMI on dedicated servers (v1.5.1): Extended Features 3.0.0 (the latest release) attaches its universal disassembler's custom recipe logic to extendedfeatures:machine_disassembly whatever its config says, and that logic reads the integrated server's level when GTM's EMI plugin builds representative recipes. On a client joined to a dedicated server it threw and aborted GTM's whole EMI plugin (no GregTech recipe in EMI; singleplayer was fine, so the singleplayer bench never saw it). The disassembler is out (2026-10-24), so startup_scripts/grindless/integration/extendedfeatures_disassembler.js clears that type's custom logic; gg_test_addon_gates_ef checks it (no_disassembler_logic). Proof: a test client joined a staging copy of the play server and the EMI coverage fixture ran.
- 2026-11-05 · Ambient and cosmetic mods out (owner, after a player's feedback: 11 fps, walking in the inventory, the item highlight): InvMove, Item Highlighter, AmbientSounds, Alex's Mobs with its library Citadel, Naturalist and Not Enough Animations. No script, table, quest, test or EMI entry referenced them (Naturalist's tortoise line leaves tools/test/log-allowlist.txt). Kept: GeckoLib (alltheleaks declares it), Iceberg (Advancement Plaques and Legendary Tooltips need it), Traveler's Titles (2026-10-24, it names the dimensions), Aquaculture (fishing), Biomes O' Plenty (removing it changes existing worlds' biomes) and the structure mods. Existing worlds lose those mods' animals.
- 2026-11-05 · Quest book FPS (owner: UHV and later chapters near 1 fps, LV and MV fine): FTB Quests 2001.4.22 draws each quest button by calling Quest#isVisible several times per frame, uncached, and with no visibility flag that call recurses through the first dependency back to the book's first quest, across earlier chapters (about 12k steps a frame on LV, 124k on UHV, 323k on MAX+). The generated era, processing-line and power chapters now set hide_quest_until_deps_visible, which sends the call to the per-team cache FTB Quests keeps for that flag (cleared only on progress). Nothing is hidden: no quest uses invisible or hiding flags and every root is visible, so the book looks the same. Chapters are not split. The client quest book test fails if any quest is not visible and notes the isVisible cost on UHV and MAX+.
